A route's request and response shapes move to the domain package's contract module as plain zod, with the worker naming components at the route; the Bindings type leaves app.ts; and the tree splits into an HTTP side and a background side held apart by an import-boundary fitness test. Four repo-wide fitness checks stop the shape regrowing (a route table proving every operation resolves to its own handler, route hygiene, per-file size and schema caps with a shrinking allow-list, and the boundary), and wrangler check startup joins the gate as a trend. Lazy per-domain loading is deferred: Hono's router refuses additions after its first match and domains do not map to path prefixes, so it needs a second composition a measurement has not yet justified. The 2026-08-21 ruling that the background orchestration stays in this Worker is kept."
- **ADR-0494** · Accepted · 2026-11-09 · Auth & access control — "A role can name a parent and so be a sub-role of it; Partner and Associate are seeded under Attorney. A member who holds a sub-role also holds the parent as its own member_role row, so every read that joins member_role to role_permission (the resolver, notification recipients, trust authority reads and triggers) sees the parent's permissions without knowing sub-roles exist, and a sub-role's own grants are only what it adds. Sub-roles add and never subtract, one level deep, at most one per parent per member; the set-roles route refuses a set that breaks either rule, and the invite path assigns the parent alongside. Time is priced by the sub-role's rate, then the parent's, then the card default, below the per-timekeeper rate. The Owner/Admin role is renamed Admin, handle unchanged."
- **ADR-0495** · Accepted · 2026-11-09 · AI / inference — "The catalog moves to the current Anthropic generation (version 4): Opus 5.5 and Sonnet 5.5 are the only selectable reasoning entries (by the owner's ruling, no previous generation is offered), each provider model records its effort levels and default, and the utility slot moves from claude-haiku-4-5 (past its earliest published retirement) to claude-haiku-5-5. The platform may now read Anthropic's live GET /v1/models with an optional read-only key, cached, and the AI settings screen reports per roster entry whether it is still published and which published models are not yet curated. The live list is information beside the roster and never a source of choices: a model becomes selectable only through a catalog entry that records its retention facts. By the owner's decision of 2026-11-09 the most-capable tier moves to Opus 5.5 and the balanced tier to Sonnet 5.5; firms that pinned a current model keep it."
- **ADR-0496** · "Accepted (amended 2026-10-02)" · 2026-09-24 · Directory — "With the owner's ruling that an address may leave the tenancy, the lookup engine was chosen, and the deciding fact was storage terms rather than coverage or price: the address a person picks is written into a permanent record, one of the two largest autocomplete services lets a customer keep only its own place identifier and the other treats every result as temporary, while Smarty lets the firm keep the data — including after cancelling. The engine is dormant until its key pair is provisioned, so no deploy waits on an account and nothing is spent until the owner opens one. The Worker serves it through two read-shaped POSTs rather than GETs, because invocation logging records every full URL and the query is a client's address typed a few characters at a time. It is gated on directory:edit, which covers both forms that ask with no migration. In the form, what is typed is the street line and nothing takes it back; a chosen address replaces the parts it carries and leaves the rest; a building with many units is opened, never chosen; and after one failure the box stops asking until somebody presses Try Again."
- **ADR-0497** · Accepted · 2026-10-02 · Directory — "Address autocomplete for the United States runs on an index the platform builds itself from OpenAddresses every month, stored in one platform R2 bucket as four census-region Tantivy splits and read by range from a Container that sleeps when nobody is typing; Smarty, unchanged, answers every other country when its key pair is set. A measured spike decided it: 62 bytes an address, one ranged read for a warm keystroke at the median, p90 under 220 ms even at 100 ms a read once result rows are fetched in parallel, and OpenStreetMap holding the house number for only 31 of 200 sampled US addresses. Sources with share-alike, non-commercial or bespoke-agreement licences are excluded; unrecorded and indemnification-only licences are included, and every build writes an attribution manifest. The engine is a dedicated address worker that, unlike the search worker, holds its own R2 binding, because address data belongs to no firm."
- **ADR-0498** · Accepted · 2026-11-09 · Dev tooling — "`pnpm gate` stays the gate of record and runs hosted again as the required `gate` check, one job in `ci.yml` that runs the same command against GitHub's merge of the branch, on an Actions budget the owner funded with a spending cap. Two workflows ported from the Grim-Games repository go with it: PR upkeep, which comments on a conflicting or stuck pull request so its thread wakes and fixes it, and Preview cleanup, which deletes leftover Worker Previews so they stop holding Durable Object namespaces. The root CLAUDE.md gains the rules that make parallel pull requests land: merge `main` before every push, check with `git merge-tree`, turn on auto-merge. Amends ADR-0478 on where the gate runs; the command stays the gate of record."
